<div class="qodef-shortcode qodef-m qodef-qi-section-title qodef-decoration--italic qodef-link--underline-draw qodef-subtitle-icon--left">
<h2 class="qodef-m-title">
WHAT IS PEPTIDE THERAPY?	</h2>
<div class="qodef-m-text"><p>Aging in both sexes leads to declines in hormones, affecting energy, sleep, mood, libido, and weight. It also causes skin to lose elasticity, increases fat, and decreases muscle and bone mass, largely due to reduced Growth Hormone (GH).</p><p> </p><p><a href="https://edgepeptide.com/anti-aging-wellness/">Peptide therapy</a> can help correct hormone imbalances and reduce aging symptoms such as low energy, decreased mental focus, reduced sex drive, lower bone density, and poor skin elasticity. Benefits include increased energy, enhanced sex drive, improved bone density, better skin elasticity, muscle tone, stamina, fuller hair, lower body fat and cholesterol, fewer wrinkles, and less joint and muscle pain. We also use peptides for wellness, chronic illness, pain management, and weight management.</p></div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<span class="sbi-screenreader">Where you get your supplements matters.
Not all s</span>
<img decoding="async" src="https://edgepeptide.com/wp-content/plugins/instagram-feed/img/placeholder.png" alt="Where you get your supplements matters.
Not all supplements are created equal — and purchasing online from unknown sources can come with real risks. At Edge Peptide Therapy, we prioritize medical-grade supplements sourced through trusted pharmacies to ensure safety, quality, and effectiveness.
Here’s why that matters 👇
⚠️ Possible dangers of buying supplements online:
• Incorrect dosing or inconsistent potency
• Contaminants or harmful fillers
• Misleading labels or undisclosed ingredients
• Poor absorption or ineffective formulations
• Expired or improperly stored products
• Drug interactions without medical guidance
• Counterfeit or imitation products
• No accountability or quality testing
Medical-grade supplements are typically:
✔️ Third-party tested
✔️ Manufactured in regulated facilities
✔️ Higher quality &amp; better absorption
✔️ Properly dosed for effectiveness
✔️ Recommended based on your health goals
✔️ Backed by medical guidance
Your health is too important to guess. Choose supplements you can trust — sourced with quality, safety, and results in mind.
To learn more, visit us at www.edgepeptide.com

<script>
const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
if( lazyloadBackground ) {
lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
}
lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
}
});
}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
} );
};
const events = [
'DOMContentLoaded',
'elementor/lazyload/observe',
];
events.forEach( ( event ) => {
document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
} );
</script>
